## 2026-10-09 ARV-104c Observer tablet screens: line tally and tracer timing (Phase 1, branch story/mvp-phase1)
- Summary: `/validation/capture` in Ariva.Web (sidebar item Validation capture under Operations, shown with `Validation.Capture`, which only the Validation observer role holds; the observer's home screen links to it). The observer chooses a running campaign of its site (sites from `/api/auth/me`), then works in two tabs that stay mounted, so a bin closes and a tracer's timer runs whichever tab is shown. Line tally: choose a line, large In and Out pads (192 to 240px high) with Undo, the bin in progress in site local time with the time left; at each quarter hour the closed bin is queued and sent (`POST .../capture/campaigns/{id}/counts`) with a random Idempotency-Key (`crypto.randomUUID`, kept with the bin for its retries). A part bin (counting started more than 5 s after the bin began, or a pause of more than 30 s between clock ticks: the tablet slept or the tab was in the background) is shown as such and never sent; Stop counting drops the bin in progress. The observer's own counts of the line are listed with Correct (in, out and a reason, its own key per correction, a hint never to name a person). Tracers: a label T-01 to T-999 (ASCII digits only, checked before anything is sent), the queue zone, Join; each tracer in a queue shows the time waited with Exit, Left unserved (abandoned) and Cancel run; each finished run is a batch of one with its own key and `deviceClockUtc` read at every send; the measured `clockOffsetMs` is shown (a warning above a minute), recorded runs are listed with the server's wait and offset. Unsent items sit in an in-memory outbox (`components/pages/validation/outbox.svelte.ts`) behind a "Not sent yet" banner with Send again now; they are also retried on the browser's `online` event and with the next send. A network failure, 408, 429 or 5xx waits for a retry; any other refusal is final and shown in plain words (403: you created or started this campaign; 400 clock: set the tablet's time to automatic and time the tracer again), with Dismiss. Leaving the screen with unsent items, a tally running or a tracer in a queue is held with Stay or Leave anyway (a reload or closed tab asks through the browser); sign-out and an ended session are never held. Roles without the permission see the no-access panel and the page asks the API nothing.
- Shared pieces for ARV-104d and ARV-104h: `src/lib/core/validation.ts` (types and calls of the capture API, `newKey`, bins, `isRetryable`, `isClockRefusal`), the generic `Outbox`, `format.ts` (site local times, offsets), `ui.ts` (touch-sized button and field classes), `OutboxBanner`. `Result<T>` in `Api.ts` now carries the HTTP `status` (absent for a network failure), so a caller can tell a refusal from a request worth sending again.
- Security (CWE-79, 384, 863): campaign, zone and line names and correction reasons are interpolated as text; no `{@html}`, no inline script or style attribute; nothing is written to web storage (the test compares local and session storage before and while a bin waits); tokens stay in memory (ARV-051); the screen is a convenience, the server decides (403 on the creator or starter, 404 outside the site). Tracer codes are labels; the field refuses names, markup and other digits.
- Tests: `functional/validation-capture.spec.ts` (9 tests at a new site E2EO with four new accounts: tally with Playwright's clock an hour back, a part bin not sent, two full bins, a lost answer after the server recorded the bin retried with the same key and stored once, storage untouched, the held navigation, a correction with a markup reason; a tracer run with a lost answer retried with the same key and a new clock reading, abandoned and cancelled runs, markup, names and other digits refused as codes; a tablet 10 minutes behind refused with the set-the-time message; the starter's plain 403; markup campaign, zone and line names as text; landscape 1280x800 and portrait 800x1280 on touch with target sizes, nothing past either edge, axe in light, dark and Arabic, keyboard Enter and Space on the pads and arrow keys on the tabs, RTL order; a terminal duty manager and an administrator without the item, the no-access panel and no validation API call); `functional/validation-observer.spec.ts` (the observer's sidebar now holds exactly Validation capture, and the home link); `functional/accessibility.spec.ts` (the screen in light, dark, Arabic and collapsed). Screenshots of each view are attached to the layout and capture tests.
- Docs: wiki 12 (Validation capture section), wiki 07 section 8 (tablet steps for counts and tracers), docs/security/cwe-controls.md (CWE-79 and CWE-384 rows).
- Gates (2026-10-09): `node scripts/verify.mjs story --specs tests/functional/validation-capture.spec.ts,tests/functional/validation-observer.spec.ts,tests/functional/accessibility.spec.ts,tests/api/validation.spec.ts` PASS on fresh services; the change is wide (`src/lib/core`, `tests/support`), so it ran in full: build, 4,083 unit tests, scanner self-test (42 rules), scan, base images, chart security, NuGet and npm audits, docs, web (svelte-check, lint, build), integration 434 passed and 1 skipped, E2E 598 passed and 1 skipped, none flaky. After the gate, Change campaign was disabled while anything is unsent or refused (review finding: the banner would have left with the campaign); web, docs and scan PASS again, and the touched specs (validation-capture, validation-observer, accessibility, shell) 62 passed and 1 skipped on fresh services. `node scripts/security/scan.mjs` 0 errors (1,166 files; the 6 pending allowlist warnings, none in the touched files).
- Security review, first verdict (2026-10-09): FAIL, three Medium and four Low. M1 (CWE-863, CWE-501): a send loop already running went on after a sign-out, so one observer's queued bins could leave under the next account's token (also through the 401 recovery, which refreshes to the next account's cookie or signs it out). M2: a correction's Idempotency-Key could be sent again with a changed body. M3: docs/security/cwe-controls.md claimed a sign-out test that did not exist. L1: a request refused before it was sent (no HTTP status) counted as retryable and blocked the queue. L2: the clock refusal was recognised by English text with nothing tying it to the C# constant. L3: no timeout on a send and no retry timer. L4: bins follow the tablet's clock (an owner decision).
- Fixed after the review (2026-10-09, not committed): (M1) each queued item carries the account that queued it (`auth.subject`, the token's subject); `Outbox.close()` stops the queue for good (closed flag, retry timer cancelled, items forgotten) and is called when the screen is destroyed and when the signed-in subject changes (sign-out, ended session, another account in another tab); `flush()` checks the flag before each send and after its await, drops items of another account before sending, and records nothing once closed; `Api.ts` takes `asSubject`: a request bound to an account is not sent, not sent again and not recovered (no refresh, no sign-in redirect) once another account or none is signed in. (M2) the correction form freezes its inputs while the outcome of a send is unknown (no answer, timeout, 408, 429, 5xx): Send again repeats the same body with the same key, Cancel reloads the counts; a definite answer unfreezes it and the next body gets a new key; a 409 for a reused key closes the form with a plain message and reloads the counts (chosen over minting a key per body, which could save a second correction when the first one's answer was lost). (M3) the test now exists (below) and the CWE-384 and CWE-863 rows describe it. (L1) `Result.status` is 0 for a request sent without an answer and absent for one refused before it was sent; only 0, 408, 429 and 5xx are retried. (L2) the fragments `clockRefusalText` and `keyReusedText` in `validation.ts`, tied to `ValidationErrors.ClockOffsetTooLarge` and `KeyReused` by the unit test `ValidationErrorTextTests` (it reads the web file; checked to fail when the web text drifts), and no other validation text contains them; the API sends no error code for them. (L3) every capture call has `AbortSignal.timeout(30 s)`; a failure worth retrying schedules a retry after 10 s, doubling to 5 minutes, reset on success. (L4) recorded in docs/product/decisions.md (Observer tablet entry, point 1, and an Open decisions row).
- Tests added after the review: `validation-capture.spec.ts` "a correction whose answer is lost is frozen and sent again as it is; a key used for another correction is refused in plain words" (inputs not editable, Send again with the same key and body, one revision stored; then a real 409 KeyReused, produced by sending the form's key with another body first, shown as the plain message with the counts reloaded); "signing out drops the unsent bins: nothing is sent afterwards, and nothing under the next account on the tablet" (two bins unsent, a retry held in flight across the sign-out and the next observer's sign-in on the same page, then answered with a success: no further count is sent, at once or on the retry timer, none carries the next account, the next account stays signed in, its tablet starts empty, web storage unchanged, Ariva holds no count of either observer; checked against the pre-fix code, where it fails with counts sent under the second observer's subject); "a send without an answer within 30 seconds is kept, then sent again on its own after the retry delay"; the tally test's retry now comes from the timer (no click), with the posts checked for one key per bin however often it was sent. New E2E account `e2e.webobserver2`. Unit `ValidationErrorTextTests` (2 tests).
- Gates after the review fixes (2026-10-09): `node scripts/verify.mjs story --specs tests/functional/validation-capture.spec.ts,tests/functional/validation-observer.spec.ts,tests/functional/accessibility.spec.ts,tests/api/validation.spec.ts` PASS on fresh services, in full again (the change is wide: `src/lib/core`, `tests/support`): build, 4,085 unit tests, scanner self-test (42 rules), scan (1,167 files, 0 errors, the 6 pending allowlist warnings), base images, chart security, NuGet and npm audits, docs, web (svelte-check, lint, build), integration 434 passed and 1 skipped, E2E 601 passed and 1 skipped, none flaky. `node scripts/security/scan.mjs --changed` 0 errors and 0 warnings (21 files); `node scripts/verify.mjs quick` PASS. Before the gate, `validation-capture.spec.ts` alone: 12 passed on fresh services.
- Security review re-check (2026-10-09): PASS. M1 to M3 and L1 to L3 confirmed fixed in the code and covered by tests (outbox owner and closed flag checked before each send and after its await; `asSubject` checked before the token is attached and before the 401 recovery; the correction body kept and resent unchanged while its outcome is unknown; the sign-out test in place and matching the CWE-384 and CWE-863 rows); L4 recorded as an owner decision. CWE-79 unchanged (plain text only, no web storage); scan 0 errors (1,167 files) rerun by the reviewer. Its remaining Low, outside this story: the general 401 recovery in `Api.ts` can still resend another screen's in-flight request under the next account signed in on the same browser (the server applies that account's permissions and audits it; the behaviour predates this story). Carried to ARV-147 (default `asSubject` to the subject at the start of every request), before go-live.
- Choices for the owner (recorded in docs/product/decisions.md, Observer tablet entry, points 1 to 6): bins follow the tablet's own clock (the tracer batches measure and correct the offset, counts do not; a tablet off by a minute shifts the bin edges by a minute; ARV-104d may align its minute grid to the server's Date header instead); a part bin is never sent (the alternative is sending it marked, which the API cannot express); one run per tracer batch (simplest retry rule; the API takes up to 20); sign-out drops unsent items without asking.
- Deferred: the desk log grid (ARV-104d) and the campaign screens (ARV-104h); an offline-capable tablet (needs an ADR); counting two lines at once.
